    "Appendix 2: Community orders and fine payment work in England and "
    "Wales"
  Sect
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
      " "
    H3 (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.533
      Span (inline)
        "Introduction"
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.291
      Span (inline)
        "1"
      " The following paragraphs describe the different types of community orders and fine payment work "
      "available in England and Wales."
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "2"
      " Although generally they only apply in England and Wales, community orders can be made by courts in "
      "England and Wales in relation to offenders residing in Scotland"
      Span (inline):
         /BaselineShift 0.099
        "1"
      "."
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.41
       /TextAlign /End
      "1 Criminal Justice Act 2003, Sch 9, para 180"
    H3 (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.533
      Span (inline)
        "Fine payment work"
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.291
      Span (inline)
        "3"
      " Fine payment work has been introduced for people who are genuinely unable to pay their fine. They are "
      "allowed to do unpaid work in the voluntary sector as an alternative. When the work is done, the fine is "
      "regarded as paid. A fines officer works out the number of hours the offender is required to work to "
      "discharge the fine. The offender is allowed to reduce the number of hours he is required to work by "
      "paying part of the sum."
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "4"
      " The work should have been arranged so as not to affect benefit entitlement or the offender’s ability to "
      "be readily available to seek or take up work."
    H3 (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.533
      Span (inline)
        "Community orders"
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.291
      Span (inline)
        "5"
      " The community order is made up of twelve requirements. The number of requirements a claimant will "
      "undertake will vary depending on the seriousness of the offence and the likelihood of them re-offending. "
      "A claimant may undertake only one of the requirements, but they could potentially undertake up to four "
      "or more."
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "Unpaid work requirement"
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "6"
      " This can last from 40 to 300 hours, and can usually be undertaken on any day of the week. It is "
      "equivalent to the community punishment order available under the old sentencing arrangements and it "
      "will vary according to the needs and circumstances of the claimant."
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "Supervision requirement"
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "7"
      " This can last for up to three years. It is similar to the current community rehabilitation order. The "
      "offender has to report regularly to a “responsible officer” (usually a probation officer). Reporting times "
      "can be flexible to reflect the offender’s other commitments such as jobsearch."
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "Programme requirement"
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "8 "
      "These are structured, accredited programmes designed to impact positively on aspects of the "
      "offender’s behaviour. The programmes can vary in length between 20 to 25 half-day sessions to 30 to "
      "60 half-day sessions depending on the nature of the offence, most are typically delivered twice weekly "
      "but this may vary in individual Probation Service areas."
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "Treatment requirement"
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "9"
      " There are three types of requirements drug rehabilitation, alcohol treatment and mental health "
      "treatment. These requirements can vary widely in length and intensity according to offender need. The "
      "claimant must get treatment for at least four hours each day. They will be required to have carried out at "
      "least 20 hours in the first 13 weeks of their sentence."
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "Residence requirement"
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "10"
      " The offender can be required to live at a specified address for a fixed period. This can be either a "
      "private address or a hostel."
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "Activity requirement"
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "11 "
      "The offender can be required to undertake any activity for up to 60 separate days. These might "
      "include activities designed to improve the offender’s skills or employability and will often include an "
      "element of basic skills."
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "Prohibited activity requirement"
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "12"
      " The offender must refrain from any activities specified by the court. The court will also specify the "
      "times and days when these activities should not be undertaken."
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "Exclusion requirement"
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "13"
      " The offender is forbidden from entering a specified place or area for up to two years. Exclusions will "
      "also be combined with rehabilitative treatment."
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "Curfew requirement"
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      Span (inline)
        "14"
      " Offenders must remain in a specified place (usually the home address) for specified periods (two to "
      "twelve hours a day or on certain days for up to six months). Generally this will be electronically monitored "
      "and they may also only be allowed in the community with supervision."
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block

        "Attendance centre requirement"
